Title: De Coelo et ejus Mirabilibus, et de Inferno, ex auditis et visis
Creator: Emanuel Swedenborg (the title page names no author)
Publisher: Londini (the title page's imprint; the host catalogue reads 'London [i.e. Amsterdam]')
Publication Date: 1758
Rights: Public Domain (published 1758, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item decoeloetejusmir00swed (https://archive.org/details/decoeloetejusmir00swed), the item's own decoeloetejusmir00swed_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign; host metadata date: 1758. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 021c50db25a77d03843eeedf17603d6160abd9ef5cd32dd4813e63e6ab84e7d8.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Illinois scan of the first edition; good OCR of the Latin, long s as f.
Content note: Swedenborg's account of heaven, the world of spirits and hell 'from things heard and seen': the Lord as the sun of heaven, the angels, the societies of heaven, the state of the dead and the life after death.
